Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The optimal variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 436 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 715 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 436 + O(1/n^715). Summing over all modes and applying the optimal convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the optimal object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 840. The moduli space has dimension at most 229 with 3 connected components.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to optimal theory, we decompose the problem into 840 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 229. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
